# Decisions

Decisions made while building Note Board, with dates and reasons, moved here from `CLAUDE.md` on 2026-10-05 so it stays short. Add new ones at the end, in the same style: date, who decided (owner request, owner rule, my call) and a plain-English sentence or two. `SPEC.md` describes the app as built; this file says why.

- 2026-10-01: Stack as in `CLAUDE.md` (spec default, no alternative). localStorage over IndexedDB: the board is small and synchronous saving is simpler and easier to test.
- 2026-10-01: Clicking the zoom percentage or Ctrl+0 resets to 100% around the center of the screen (not back to the board's top-left like the prototype).
- 2026-10-01: Toolbar buttons for later steps are shown from step 1 but do nothing until their step (Undo/Redo/Color faded).
- 2026-10-01 (step 2): New loose cards and new columns appear at the free spot nearest the middle of the screen.
- 2026-10-01 (step 2): A card drops into a column when the pointer is over the column (or up to 60px below it), as in the prototype.
- 2026-10-01 (step 2): Text on cards is always black (#1F1D1A) whatever the card color, including done items, the link address and the "Open …" button. The palette's Text colors are not used for card text.
- 2026-10-01 (step 3): Resize limits follow the prototype: cards 200–640 wide, columns 240–640 wide, heights 100–700. When clearing overlaps, the block that just moved/grew/was resized stays put, then columns, then loose cards move out of the way.
- 2026-10-01 (step 4): Pasted blocks keep their spot and whatever they would cover moves (spec wording). The prototype did the opposite (the pasted copy moved away); flip by making pasted ids non-anchors in `placeCopies`.
- 2026-10-01 (step 4): A card copied from inside a column is pasted back into that column, right below the original (as in the prototype). Ctrl+D duplicates without replacing what Ctrl+C copied.
- 2026-10-01 (step 4): Several selected blocks dragged together don't drop into columns and show no landing outline; after the drop, anything they cover moves.
- 2026-10-01 (step 4): Delete with several columns selected asks "Delete N columns?" on the first selected column.
- 2026-10-01 (step 4): Automatic overlap clean-up is part of the change that caused it, not a separate undo step. Undo covers board data only (not pan, zoom, tool or selection).
- 2026-10-01 (step 5): Behavior follows the prototype where the spec is silent: Enter adds the new item after the current one's sub-items (replaced 2026-10-02, see below); a top-level item only nests (Tab) under an item in the same section (open or completed); nesting by drag puts items first under the target.
- 2026-10-01 (step 5): Dropping dragged items back onto themselves does nothing (the prototype appended them to the end of their list). An item drag starts after a 5px nudge, like block drags.
- 2026-10-01 (step 5): A new list made by dropping items on the board keeps its spot; anything under it moves out of the way. Whether "Completed" is open is saved per list.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request, beyond the spec): Add Note / To-do list / Link can be dragged from the toolbar onto the board, and so can New column. The new block appears at the nearest free spot to the pointer; a new card over a column goes into it (a new column never does).
- 2026-10-01 (owner request, changes the spec): The trash can shows on hover for every checklist item, not only completed ones.
- 2026-10-01 (owner rule, beyond the spec): Deleting a blank checklist item (trash, Backspace, Delete) keeps its sub-items when any of them has text: they move up one level into its place. Otherwise, and for items with text, everything under the item is deleted. Cut (Ctrl+X) always removes sub-items, since they were copied with the parent. Backspace therefore now works in a blank item with sub-items.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request, beyond the spec): Up / Down arrows move between checklist items in the same list (Up → end of the item above, Down → start of the item below), only from an item's top / bottom line so multi-line items still move line by line. Shift+arrows keep selecting text. Hidden completed items are skipped.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request, beyond the spec): "Scrolling" between cards was taken to mean the arrow keys. Up / Down move through all of a card's text fields and, inside a column, on into the next card (selecting it); collapsed cards are skipped; loose cards don't continue to other cards. Alt + arrows (changed from Ctrl + arrows at the owner's request, so Ctrl + arrows keep moving word by word in text) jump to the nearest card in that direction by on-screen position, select it, focus its first field (cursor at the end) and pan it into view. With nothing selected they start from the card nearest the middle of the screen. Alt + arrows are always used by the board, so Alt + ← / → never trigger the browser's Back / Forward.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request: snapping felt rigid): Snapping is applied where a block lands, not while it moves. During a drag the block follows the pointer exactly and the landing outline shows the snapped, free spot; during a resize the drawn size follows the pointer (`liveW`/`liveH`) and the label shows the snapped size; size matches still click in live. Loose blocks and columns glide (160ms) to new positions and sizes, except while following the pointer; no gliding with prefers-reduced-motion. Browser tests run with reduced motion, apart from `e2e/extras-smooth.spec.ts`.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request, changes the spec): The dragged block takes priority. Instead of landing at the nearest free spot (spec), it lands on the grid spot under the pointer and the blocks in its way move to the nearest free spot; the move is previewed live (`Drag.bumped`) and the drop commits exactly that preview (`dropResult` in the store). Exception: a single dragged card never pushes columns (they are anchored), so dragging a card towards a column still drops it in; over a column without dropping in, the card takes the nearest free spot. Group drags and column drags push everything. New blocks from the toolbar still go to the nearest free spot.
- 2026-10-01: Owner approved committing each step on branch `build/v1` and continuing through step 5 without pausing for manual checks.
- 2026-10-01: Ctrl+= / Ctrl+− / Ctrl+0 also work while typing, so the browser never zooms the whole page instead. Pan and zoom are remembered between visits; the tool always starts as Hand.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request): Repo made public and published on GitHub Pages (https://ezhang43.github.io/note-board/) by `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` on every push to `build/v1` (unit tests must pass). Vite's `base` is `/note-board/` for builds only. When work moves to `main`, change the workflow branch and the `github-pages` environment's allowed branches.
- 2026-10-01 (owner request, beyond the spec): The published site syncs the board through Firebase (project `note-board-a672a`): Google sign-in, one Firestore document `boards/{uid}` holding the same JSON as localStorage. Only `ezhang43@gmail.com` may read/write (`firestore.rules`, pasted into the Firebase console by the owner; not deployed from here). Signed out, the site shows only a sign-in screen. First sign-in: the online board wins; if there is none, this device's board is uploaded. Afterwards the most recent change wins; a change from another device resets undo and waits for any drag to finish (`src/store/sync.ts`). Sync is on only when `VITE_SYNC=on` (`.env.production`), so dev and tests stay local-only. A service worker (`public/sw.js`) lets the published page open offline; Firestore's offline cache keeps the data.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, beyond the spec): Import a Milanote board exported as Markdown (Import button; parser in `src/model/milanote.ts`). Owner's answers: a heading is a to-do list title (an empty heading is an empty list), a blank line between items starts a new untitled list, cards go loose in a tidy grid (no columns can be recovered from the flat export), imports add to the current board. My calls: the "# board name" line is ignored; text under a heading is a note starting with the heading; a lone web address is a link card; layout is 4 lanes (shortest lane first), re-tidied once real heights are known; imported cards are selected and one undo removes them. The owner's real export stays out of git (public repo); tests use `e2e/fixtures/milanote-sample.md`.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, changes the spec): Cards are always white; only columns are colored, and the Color button only recolors columns. Cards keep their saved `color` field (unused) so old boards still load. No "2/5 done" count. Column collapse arrow moved to the right, next to ×. Deleting stays as it was: columns ask first (×, Delete or Backspace), single cards never do.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, beyond the spec): Palette grows to 16 colors (owner chose 16). Auto-color button: every column gets a different color (owner: columns only), left to right, in a hue-spread order; repeats only past 16 columns. My calls: the 8 new colors and their order, button placed right after Color.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, beyond the spec): Delete at the end of an item joins the next shown item into it; that item's sub-items move up one level into its place (same idea as the blank-item rule); never across open → Completed. Tab / Shift+Tab with several items selected moves each one that can one level (changed from all-or-nothing after the owner found Tab did nothing when the first item was selected), selection kept.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, beyond the spec): Collapsing a card/column returns the blocks its expansion pushed aside. The store remembers pushes from settles in the 1.5s after an expand (`pushedBy`, not saved); a block only returns if it hasn't moved since and its old spot is free.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, beyond the spec): Clean up + master Completed card (card kind `completed`, `src/model/completed.ts`). Owner's answers: a special card on the board grouped by date; everything ticked moves (top-level and sub-items); unticking restores to the source list. My calls: newest day first; a ticked item takes its whole subtree; restore goes under the old parent if it still exists (else end of list; else a new list with the old title next to the Completed card); the card can be moved/collapsed/resized/put in a column but never deleted or copied (deleting its column leaves it loose); Clean up is undoable. Saved shape stays version 2 (an extra card kind). Toolbar spacing tightened so it fits at 1280px.
- 2026-10-02 (owner requests): Columns with cards fit their cards (owner chose this over keeping resized heights); `col.h` only applies to an empty column. Collapsed loose cards resize width only, from a right-edge strip (my call). Growth pushes blocks below straight down (owner: always, not only on expand): `settle(..., pushDown)` from the store's growth settles; drag drops keep nearest-free-spot.
- 2026-10-02 (owner requests): All sub-items ticked → the item ticks (upwards); my call: unticking a sub-item unticks its ancestors. Completing animation: the store delays a tick that sends items to Completed by 280ms (`ui.completing`), then marks them `arrived` for 450ms; any other change, undo or redo applies a waiting tick first; no delay with reduced motion (so unit tests and most browser tests see ticks at once). No count on "Completed".
- 2026-10-02 (owner requests): Column title + count centered (3-column grid header). Card titles get a tinted band (owner chose this over teal text or underline): header + title of to-do, link and Completed cards, `--band` = accent-soft loose; inside a column (owner: the column's own color was hard to see) `color-mix(--col-edge 70%, --bg)`, a deeper shade of the column color.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request): Copying/cutting selected items also writes plain text to the system clipboard, one item per line; my call: sub-items indented two spaces per level, no tick marks.
- 2026-10-02 (owner bug reports): Copy/cut take exactly the highlighted items (`copyItems` prunes unselected sub-items, `removeExactly` for cut, `selectionAsText` indents relative to the shallowest selected item). Shift+click with no selection starts from the focused item. Known limit: a first sub-item can't move in a level (nothing above it to nest under); in a mixed selection it stays while the rest move.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request): Color with cards selected sets `titleColor` (new optional card field) for the title band; band = color-mix(edge 70%, bg) of that swatch, inline `--band`. My calls: notes get the band on their header strip; a "Default" choice in the menu resets (columns to gray, cards to the usual band).
- 2026-10-02 (owner requests): Collapse all / Expand all toggle (icon button after Auto-color, so the toolbar still fits at 1280px): collapses everything if anything is open, else expands all; one undo step; forgets remembered pushes. Collapsed columns resize width from their right edge; collapsed blocks tint that edge on hover (my call, since it is their only handle).
- 2026-10-02 (owner rule): Tests first — write the tests for every change and see them fail before implementing it (build rule 3).
- 2026-10-02 (owner requests): Arrow keys move the selected blocks one grid step (Shift: five), like a drag (they keep their spot, the rest moves; a card can push a column). My calls: a single selected card in a column moves up / down its column instead; with checklist items selected the arrows do nothing; while typing the arrows stay in the text, and Escape in a card or column field blurs it (block stays selected) so the arrows then move it; quick presses merge into one undo step. Collapsed loose cards center their title (in-column cards unchanged, as the owner said single cards).
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, beyond the spec): Dark mode with a toolbar toggle. Owner's answers: a first visit follows the computer's setting; the choice is per device (`note-board:theme`, not synced, not undoable; `view.theme`); columns become deep, muted shades (`DARK_PALETTE`). My calls: a moon icon button at the right end (after Clean up); in dark mode cards are dark gray with light text (this replaces "card text is always black" for dark mode only); the page gets `<html data-theme>` from the store, so the sign-in screen follows too; the browser title-bar color follows; the shades are tested for contrast.
- 2026-10-02 (code review fixes, sync): A local change not yet uploaded, or a drop made after another device saved, wins over the incoming version (it is sent instead; the other device's version is dropped). Received versions are compared in this page's own save format, so nothing is echoed back. An online board that can't be read (damaged or newer version, `readBoard` returns null) is never applied or overwritten: sync stops and the existing "couldn't be loaded" screen shows.
- 2026-10-02 (owner choice, beyond the spec): A refused upload shows a banner "Couldn't save online. Changes are on this device only." (owner chose a banner over retrying silently); the next change retries; while it stands, incoming versions are ignored so Firestore's rollback can't wipe local changes. Uploads also flush when the page is hidden (owner: yes), via `src/sync/pageHide.ts`.
- 2026-10-02 (code review fixes, my calls): Delete / Backspace do nothing while a block is being dragged or resized. A Ctrl-held selection box clears the checklist item selection (Ctrl+A did too, until the 2026-10-03 Ctrl+A ladder: with items selected it now selects more items). A delayed tick is re-applied to the list as it is when it lands; every action works out its change inside `commit`'s function (which may return the board, the board plus ui, or null), so the waiting tick is always applied first.
- 2026-10-02 (code review fixes, my calls): Pasted checklist items that would pass 6 levels go after the nearest item above that leaves room. Restoring from the Completed card unticks the items it goes back under. A card copied from a column remembers the column's position, used if the column is gone at paste time. Shortcuts match the key position (`e.code`) when the typed key isn't a Latin letter (`src/model/keys.ts`). Milanote: closing #s need a space before them. Left as is: Alt+arrows also take Option+arrows on a Mac (owner works on Windows).
- 2026-10-02 (architecture review, owner OK): Drop preview and drop (`dropBoard`), remembered pushes (`recordPushes`) and returning them on collapse (`returnPushes`) are pure functions in `src/model/placement.ts`; the store keeps only the `pushedBy` map and the expand timer.
- 2026-10-02 (Impeccable review, owner approved all 11 changes): New notes / links / columns take the cursor (`ui.focusBlock`, `useTakeFocus`); my call: a new column's "New column" title is selected so typing replaces it.
- 2026-10-02 (Impeccable review): Pressed toolbar buttons all share Snap's pressed look; placeholders darkened; faded buttons 0.55 / 0.5 in dark mode; my call for the uncolored-column band: the loose-card teal band (not a new neutral).
- 2026-10-02 (Impeccable review): Toolbar grouped by three dividers (after Redo, after New column, plus the one before Sign out); Add Note / To-do list / Link plain (their old card colors are gone since cards are white); Auto-color, Collapse all, Import, Clean up are "quiet" (borderless until hover); board name min 140px. Order unchanged and still fits 1280px.
- 2026-10-02 (Impeccable review): Checklist grip and trash overlay the row's first line on hover (absolute, with a 56px fade) instead of reserving width; the hover-trash-on-every-item decision is unchanged.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, changes the spec): Enter in a checklist item works like a text editor (`enterItem` in `src/model/checklist.ts`): split at the cursor, blank item above when at the start of a non-empty item, new item directly below at the end. Owner's answer: "directly below" an item with sub-items means its first sub-item. My calls: on a split, sub-items stay with the first half and the second half goes directly below in the same way; a split ticked item stays ticked in both halves; a selection is removed first.
- 2026-10-02 (owner request, changes the spec): Collapsed loose cards can be made taller (owner chose height over in-column resizing): a corner handle on collapsed cards, height stored apart as optional `collapsedH` (saved, version unchanged) so the open height is kept; min 40px (`COLLAPSED_MIN_H`). My calls: applies to every collapsed loose card, not only to-do lists, so all collapsed cards behave the same; the header sits in the middle of a taller collapsed card.
- 2026-10-03 (second Impeccable review, owner approved all five): Board name shrinks first (min 140px, …) so buttons never leave a 1280px toolbar; Open link faded without an address; wider hover fade; one space dropped when Enter splits at a gap; 32px swatches, board pans down if the Color menu covers the selection; Keyboard shortcuts panel (`ui.shortcutsOpen`). My calls: the ? button sits beside the zoom control (bottom right) so the toolbar still fits 1280px; panel text is a fixed list in `ShortcutsPanel.tsx`.
- 2026-10-03 (owner interview, grill-with-docs round 1; owner took every recommendation): (1) App name BusyAnts in the tab title, installed-app name/icon and sign-in screen; the site address stays /note-board/. (2) Icon: a friendly ant on warm yellow, drawn by me. (3) Ctrl+A ladder: item text → every item in that list → every item in every list in the same column (skipped for a loose list) → every item on the board; Escape or clicking empty board clears. (4) Items selected across several lists: Copy, Delete and tick work; Tab and drag do not. (5) Copying items from several lists: each list title on its own line, its items indented under it. (6) Press-and-drag item selection carries on into the next cards of the same column only. (7) Reaching the screen edge pans the board during item selection, the selection box, block drags and item drags. (8) Hand tool: open glove over empty board, closed glove while panning; normal arrow over cards and columns (changes build rule 6 for the Hand tool on empty board only). (9) Same-width toolbar button next to Collapse all, faded with fewer than two blocks selected: every selected loose card and column takes the width of the first selected; one undo step. (10) Font size: A− / A+ by the zoom control, 4 steps (Small, Normal, Large, Larger), card and column text only, remembered per device. (11) Two-finger pinch zoom and pan on touch screens; no phone layout (still not in version 1). (12) Expand all puts every block back where it was before Collapse all, unless moved in between; anything in the way goes straight down. (13) Alignment guides while dragging: edges and centers of nearby blocks, snapping within 8px and winning over the grid. (14) Empty-board hint, gone once anything is on the board. (15) Published site only: "Saving…" / "Saved" note by the zoom control; the red banner on failure. (16) New lists and columns start with an empty title and a placeholder instead of "New list" / "New column".
- 2026-10-03 (owner interview, round 2; owner took every recommendation): A new to-do list puts the cursor in its (empty) title, and Enter there moves to the first item. Ctrl+A on the whole board only takes items you can see (not in collapsed cards or columns, nor in a closed Completed section). Text copied from several lists is in board order: columns left to right, each top to bottom, then loose lists top to bottom, left to right. Alignment guides are for dragging only (resizing keeps its size matching). Same width: the first selected loose card or column sets the width; cards inside columns are left alone. The published site shows "Offline. Will save when you're back online" instead of "Saving…" when there is no connection.
- 2026-10-03 (building the interview decisions, my calls): Expand all restores which blocks were open as well as positions (cards collapsed before Collapse all stay collapsed; otherwise they grew and pushed their neighbors, the likely cause of the owner's ruined layout). Same width is an icon button; narrow-window toolbar padding fixed (it never applied) so it still fits 1280px. Owner asked for a simpler, chibi icon with no face: big round head, small body, antennae, stubby legs.
- 2026-10-03 (building the interview decisions, my calls): Ctrl+A with only some of a list's items selected takes the whole list first. Across several lists Ctrl+X and Ctrl+V do nothing, like Tab and dragging. A list emptied by a delete across lists keeps one blank item, as for a single list.
- 2026-10-03 (building the interview decisions, my calls): Pinch works over cards too; once a second finger lands, whatever the first one started (pan, selection box, block or item drag) is canceled. The pinch is worked out from where it began, so fingers reporting moves one at a time don't make the board drift.
- 2026-10-03 (accessibility and wording reviews, owner approved all of it): tick boxes keep a focus ring (they aren't text fields); resize handles take arrow keys (20px, Shift 100px, one undo step per press); cards and columns are named for screen readers with their titles; 24px pressable areas for tick boxes, resize handles and the grip; the board area is a "Board" landmark; Ctrl+Shift+Up / Down move a checklist item past its neighbor (same level, never across open / Completed). Wording: column delete question names the cards that go with it and has a Keep button; "Add an item", "Paste a link address", "Drop to make a new list", "cards and columns" instead of "blocks", the empty-board hint mentions ?, the wrong-account and save-failed messages say what to do next.
- 2026-10-04 (owner bug report, replaces my 2026-10-03 call): Expand all opens every card and column, even ones collapsed before Collapse all (the owner saw loose cards and columns stay shut). Positions are still restored. While the opened blocks grow, the higher block wins so others only move down.
- 2026-10-04 (owner interview, text formatting): whole text box formatting (owner chose this over single words, which would mean rebuilding every text box as a rich-text editor): size Small / Normal / Large, bold, italic, and five typefaces (Plex Sans, Serif, Rounded, Handwritten, Typewriter); no colors. A pop-up bar appears when text in a box is highlighted (or over selected checklist items); no toolbar buttons. Ctrl+B / Ctrl+I / Ctrl+Shift+> / Ctrl+Shift+< while typing, on selected items, or on every box in selected cards. Saved, synced, undoable, kept by copy / split / Completed; text copied to other apps stays plain. My calls: fonts Lora (serif), Nunito (rounded), Caveat (handwritten), IBM Plex Mono (typewriter), all from Google Fonts; Small 85% / Large 130%; joining two items keeps the first item's formatting (a box has one format); bold / italic on several boxes turns on for all unless all have it; size keys step from the first box's size.
- 2026-10-04 (owner requests): Collapse all / Expand all act on the selection when anything is selected (the tooltip says so), else on everything. Collapsing closes the gaps: blocks below move straight up by the height the block directly above them lost, keeping their old spacing (`closeGaps` in `src/model/layout.ts`, applied while the collapsed heights come in). My calls: Expand all only gives back the earlier layout when it is for the same blocks Collapse all was; only blocks directly above count (none in between).
- 2026-10-04 (owner bug report: blocks moved by themselves a few seconds after Collapse all): a board received from another device is not re-arranged straight away, since this device's known heights are from before it (a background window, or one not yet redrawn, pushed the closed-up blocks back down and synced that back). It is only tidied once blocks have been redrawn here.
- 2026-10-04 (owner request): collapsing a single card or column with its arrow also closes the space below it, like Collapse all; this replaces "pushed blocks go back to their old spot" where that spot would leave empty space (they go back to where they sat under it).
- 2026-10-04 (owner request, replaces the 2026-10-02 Impeccable overlay): checklist grip and trash must never cover the text. Owner chose "always reserve": every item row keeps a 40px strip on the right (`.todo-item` padding-right), so text never re-wraps on hover; the fade behind the buttons is gone.
- 2026-10-04 (owner bug report): dragging items onto a list to append now outlines the whole card (the ring on `.todo-body` was hidden under the title band at the top). My call: dashed, like a column taking a card, so it never looks like the solid ring of a selected card (the source card is often selected).
- 2026-10-04 (owner request, changes "no phone layout in version 1"; owner took every recommendation): phone and touch support in four steps. (1) Touch screens (`hover: none`): only the item being typed in shows its grip and trash; the rest are hidden outright so they can't be tapped. (2) Narrow screens (under 600px wide) get a phone layout: slim top bar with the board name; bottom bar with Undo, Redo, a big + (Note / To-do list / Link / Column) and ⋯ for everything else. Tablets keep the desktop layout. (3) While typing in a checklist item on a phone, a bar above the keyboard: indent, outdent, move up / down, tick, delete. (4) Phone-sized cards and text, tap targets, keyboard not covering the item. My call (1): the trash keeps the cursor in the item when pressed, so it stays shown until tapped.
- 2026-10-04 (owner bug report: cards could not be dragged by finger; columns could): touch screens get press-and-hold (450ms) anywhere on a card, text included, then move to drag (`blockHoldPointerDown`, a capture handler so checklist rows can't swallow it). My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): the whole card including checklist items, items still move by their grip; moving before the hold cancels it; a short vibration where the phone supports it; the long-press copy / paste menu is blocked during the hold.
- 2026-10-04 (owner request, replaces "pan and zoom are remembered"): opening the board shows every card and column (`viewShowing` in `src/model/view.ts`, run by Canvas two frames after it opens, and once more when the online copy first arrives on an empty screen). My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): keep the remembered zoom when everything fits, else zoom out just enough but not below 50%; a direction that still doesn't fit shows the top / left of the board; 40px margin. Zoom is still remembered; pan is not used on opening.
- 2026-10-04 (owner request): on touch, a finger moving straight away over a card, column or resize handle pans the board; resting 450ms first drags or resizes (`holdOrPan` in `src/components/touchHold.ts`). My calls: columns follow the same rule as cards; the item grip still drags at once (it is an explicit handle shown only on the item being typed in); finger range-selection of checklist items is dropped on touch since a finger move now pans.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request, from the competitor research: quick wins, then more boards, due dates, arrows; no pictures): Backup. My calls: a File menu replaces Import (Download backup = the saved JSON, Download as text = Markdown, Restore from backup…, Import from Milanote…) so the toolbar still fits 1280px; restoring asks first, saves the current board as a version, is one undo step, and brings the board into view; iPhone home-screen apps use the Share sheet.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request, quick wins): Uncheck all, Fit to screen, clickable links. My calls: Uncheck all sits beside "Completed" (only shown when something is ticked) and unticks sub-items too; Fit to screen is the first button in the zoom control and Shift+1 (as in Figma / Miro), zooming 30–100%; a text box can't hold real links, so a note lists its addresses as links underneath (like Google Keep) and Ctrl+click opens an address in a note or item.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request): alignment guides also for several blocks dragged together. My call: they line up as one (the box around them all: its outer edges and middle), like Google Slides, rather than each block lining up on its own.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request): invite-only. `firestore.rules` lists the Google accounts let in (owner, erikazhu95@gmail.com); each gets its own board (`boards/{uid}`, as before), pasted into the Firebase console by the owner. Accounts not on the list see "BusyAnts is invite-only…". Safer publishing: the site is built from `main`, moved up to `build/v1` only when the owner says "publish" (build rule 9). My calls: no separate preview site for build/v1 (one origin would share the offline copy and the online board, so a test copy could damage real boards); the owner checks before publishing by asking for a report or running it locally.
- 2026-10-05 (code review of the cloud session's version history, fixes): versions are decided without going online (the list is read only when one may be due); a version is due when editing starts after 10 minutes without an edit, or hourly while editing; undo / redo count as edits, automatic re-arranging doesn't; a failed save is retried after a minute with the board from before; versions carry a fingerprint so the newest needn't be downloaded to compare; 20 kept on a device alone (100 online); a gesture ending while an old version is shown clears its outline / label.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request: app and toolbar should reflect the icon's yellow): honey toolbar #FFF1C2 with the ant logo before the board name, cream canvas #FAF6EC, accent teal → amber-brown #8A5A00 (dark: honey #F2C14E on a #2A2416 toolbar). My calls: a soft honey toolbar rather than the icon's full #FFCF3F yellow (calmer to sit under all day, keeps text contrast); the accent moves to amber everywhere (bands, selection, pressed buttons) so nothing teal is left to clash; checkbox green, delete red and the column swatches (Teal included) are unchanged; the title bar color follows.
- 2026-10-05 (owner bug report): the selection box sat above the pointer because the browser scrolled the board area to keep a text cursor in view; any such scroll is turned into a board pan (Canvas). Browser tests now retry once and keep a trace, screenshot and video of failures (owner asked for Playwright debugging).
- 2026-10-05 (owner request): code review is part of every change (build rule 10). My calls: after the tests pass and before pushing; also on pull requests from other sessions, where findings are reported rather than fixed; wording-only and document-only changes skip it. Owner then chose to size it by the change (light / standard / thorough) to save tokens.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request): only the main session (this computer) pushes to `build/v1`; any other session works on its own branch and opens a pull request, which the main session tests before the owner merges it (build rule 9). Prompted by the cloud session's phone changes reaching the live site unannounced.
- 2026-10-04 (owner request): collapsed loose cards start as tall as a collapsed column (60px). My calls: cards inside columns stay compact; a collapsed height set by hand (down to 40px) still wins.
- 2026-10-04 (owner request, like Google Docs): version history (`src/model/versions.ts` rules, `src/store/versions.ts` recorder / restore / local store, `versionsRemote` in `src/sync/firebase.ts`, `HistoryPanel.tsx`). My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): a version is the board just before the first change after 10 minutes without one (so the end of each editing session is kept; the live board is "Current version"); only changes made on this page count (`outsideChanges` in core); duplicates of the newest version are skipped; newest 100 kept; online in `boards/{uid}/versions` (list) + `versionData` (boards), so the list loads quickly; firestore.rules now cover everything under `boards/{uid}` (the owner pastes them into the console; until then the panel says it isn't switched on and saving versions fails quietly). Preview shows the old board through `useAppState` (`ui.preview`) while the real board stays untouched and syncing; `commit`, undo / redo and height measuring are off during a preview. Restore saves the current board first, then is one undoable change. No named versions (not asked).
- 2026-10-04 (owner request): search (`src/model/search.ts`, `FindBar.tsx`, `FindMarks.tsx`). My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): Ctrl+F takes over from the browser's find (which can't move the board), also while typing; every occurrence counts as a match; board order as for copying across lists; marks drawn as an invisible copy of the text behind each text box (`data-find` keys), current orange, others yellow; the board pans the current match to the middle of what is visible (above a phone keyboard) and zooms to 100% when below 60%; matches in collapsed cards / columns / closed Completed sections ring the card or column instead of opening it (opening would change and sync the board); link addresses ring the whole field. The board name isn't searched.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request, replaces invite-only): open to the public. Any verified Google account gets its own private board (`boards/{uid}`, as before); the sign-in screen says only you can see it. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): the rules only accept the fields the app writes and cap a board or version at 900,000 characters (Firestore's limit is 1 MiB); no App Check for now (it needs a reCAPTCHA key set up in the console by the owner); rules are checked by text tests, not an emulator.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request, from the competitor research): several boards, and boards inside boards. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): every board is saved together as one "workspace" (`src/model/workspace.ts`, saved shape version 3: { home, boards }), in the same localStorage key and the same Firestore document, so syncing stays one document with "most recent wins" (an older single-board save, or one sent by an older app, becomes / only replaces the home board); the open board is `state.board` as before, the rest in `state.boards.others`; which board is open is per device (`note-board:open`, not synced); undo is per board (kept in memory while switching); a board card (card kind `board`, `boardId`) opens a board inside another, Milanote style, with no title of its own (the board's name shows); the Boards menu is an icon button at the toolbar's left so it still fits 1280px; a new empty unnamed board no card opens is dropped on leaving it; deleting a board saves a version first, removes its cards everywhere and keeps the boards inside it; deleting a board card keeps the board; versions hold every board, restoring puts back the open board and any deleted board; a backup holds every board, and restoring one of several boards replaces every board (not undoable, versioned first). Not built: dragging cards onto a board card to move them (cut and paste works across boards).
- 2026-10-05 (code review of several boards, fixes): on first sign-in an online board from an older app replaces this device's boards outright (they may be another account's on a shared device); deleting a board or restoring a backup waits at most 3 seconds for its safety version (offline, Firestore only answers once back online), then goes ahead; a version from before several boards is of the home board, so restoring it opens home; opening a board forgets search's current match. Left as is: syncing all boards as one document (edits to two boards on two devices within a second: the later upload wins), and an unused new board is only dropped when another board is opened.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request, from the competitor research): due dates. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): on checklist items only (not notes or whole lists); a day with no time and no reminders or notifications (a web page can't remind reliably when closed); the hover strip grows from 40px to 64px so the calendar button fits beside grip and trash; the chip sits under the item's text so a narrow card keeps its text width; the picker offers Today / Tomorrow / Next week / any day / No due date; the Due panel lists unticked items due today or overdue on every board (not later ones), opened from a calendar button by the zoom control with a red count, and shares the right-hand spot with Version history (one at a time); clicking an entry opens its board and puts the cursor in the item.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request, from the competitor research): arrows between cards. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): an arrow joins two cards or columns (cards in columns too), plain with an arrowhead and no label, color or line styles; drawn by dragging a round handle that shows on the right edge of a single selected block (no new toolbar button, so the toolbar still fits 1280px); straight line between the blocks' middles, clipped at their edges, worked out from where they are drawn so it follows drags, glides and columns; at most one arrow between the same two blocks; a column and a card inside it can't be joined; selecting an arrow lets go of blocks; arrows aren't copied with cards (copy / duplicate / paste); stored as an optional `arrows` list on the board (save version unchanged, so older copies of the site still open boards but would drop arrows if they saved).

Icon PNGs are made from `public/icon.svg` by `node scripts/make-icons.mjs` (renamed `busyants-*` on 2026-10-05, below).
- 2026-10-05 (owner request): donate button through Buy Me a Coffee (https://buymeacoffee.com/ezcookie). Owner chose bottom right: a round coffee-cup button right of ?, so the toolbar still fits 1280px; on a phone, a line in the ⋯ menu. - 2026-10-05 (owner request: pull requests should go through without asking the main session each time): automatic checks on every pull request into `build/v1` (`.github/workflows/checks.yml`: typecheck, unit tests, browser tests; check name "All tests"), merging blocked until they pass (a branch ruleset the owner sets up on GitHub), and auto-merge for low-risk pull requests. Pull requests touching saving, sync, undo or deleting still wait for the main session's check and the owner's OK. My calls: also run on pushes to `build/v1` so the main session's own pushes show pass / fail; merge commits as before; failed browser tests upload their traces for 7 days; publishing to `main` stays manual.
- 2026-10-05 (owner chose the recommendation): low-risk pull requests are merged by the session that opened them once "All tests" passes, instead of relying on auto-merge, which GitHub refuses to turn on while tests are running. The `build/v1` ruleset still blocks merging anything whose tests fail.
- 2026-10-05 (owner requests): (1) the installed app still showed the old icon although the site's files already held the ant, so the icon files got new names (`busyants-*`; browsers and installed apps keep an icon by its address), plus an iPhone / iPad home-screen icon (`busyants-apple-180.png`) and a new offline-copy version. My call: an app installed before may still need removing and installing again if Windows keeps its own copy of the picture. (2) No Add Link anywhere (toolbar, phone + menu): new cards are notes and to-do lists only. My calls: link cards already on boards keep working and showing, and a Milanote import still makes a link card from a lone web address (nothing is lost or changed in saved boards). (3) "Add a board here" reads "Add a sub-board here"; my call: on a phone + menu, "Board" reads "Sub-board" to match. (4) Alt + arrows went sideways or skipped places: a card or column now counts only if it lies wholly in that direction, ones straight ahead come first, and columns are stops at their title strip (so empty and collapsed columns can be reached, and ↑ from a column's top card goes to its title). My call: plain ↑ / ↓ inside a card stay as they were.
- 2026-10-05 (owner chose this): auto-publish for low-risk pull requests. After "All tests" passes on a push to `build/v1`, a Publish job (in `checks.yml`, so it runs right after the tests) fast-forwards `main` and starts `deploy.yml` with `workflow_dispatch` (a push made with the job's own token doesn't start it). It publishes only when every commit `main` lacks (on `build/v1`'s own line) is the merge of a pull request into `build/v1` with no `high-risk` label and no risky file: `src/sync/`, `src/store/core.ts`, `src/store/sync.ts`, `src/store/versions.ts`, `src/store/actions/boards.ts`, `src/model/persist.ts`, `history.ts`, `versions.ts`, `workspace.ts`, `firestore.rules` (test files don't count). My calls: every unpublished commit is checked, not just the newest, since publishing a commit takes all earlier ones live too; anything the job can't find out means "don't publish"; the rules are in `scripts/publish-rules.mjs` with unit tests (Vitest now also runs `scripts/**/*.test.ts`); a newer push cancels an older run's tests but never a publish under way; the `high-risk` label exists on GitHub. Checked: nothing protects `main` from the job's push and the Pages environment allows `main`. Found: the `build/v1` ruleset that should block merging with failing tests is switched off (Disabled), so for now only the sessions' own discipline stops that.
- 2026-10-05 (owner requests): (1) Sign out was cut off at the right of the toolbar: the toolbar had grown past the window (the board area's grid let it), and the path to a sub-board never shrank. Fixed: the toolbar is never wider than the window, the path shrinks like the board name, the tighter spacing starts at 1500px (was 1320px, where buttons were already cut off) with 3px gaps, and Sign out is an icon button (arrow leaving a door, tooltip "Sign out"); the phone ⋯ menu keeps the words. `npm run dev` with `?signed-in` shows Sign out locally so the fit can be tested. (2) "To-do list" is called "Checklist" everywhere it shows (toolbar, phone + menu, screen-reader names, empty-board hint, drag label, app description); the saved card kind stays `todo`, so saved boards are unchanged. (3) Arrows drawn as in Miro: four dots, one just outside the middle of each side, on the hovered block (or a single selected one; on touch, the selected one), dragged onto another block; the arrow is a curve between the middles of the two facing sides (left / right when further apart sideways, else top / bottom), leaving and arriving square. My calls: the curved line style (Miro's default look for connectors from dots), no line-style options, dropping on empty board still draws nothing (Miro would offer to make a new shape there), a click on a dot without dragging draws nothing, the dots stay while the pointer crosses the column a card is in, and a dot over another card gives way to it (code review). (4) Each list has its own Clean up, just left of Uncheck all, moving only that list's ticked items into the Completed card. My calls: shown whenever Uncheck all is (the list has a Completed section); the list stays selected (the toolbar's Clean up selects the Completed card); the two buttons wrap under "Completed" on a card too narrow for all three.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request: real-time collaboration, several people editing at once; owner's answers): share by link (anyone signed in with the link can edit; chosen over invite-by-email); invited people edit everything, the sharer alone manages people, renames / deletes the board and restores versions; sharing a board shares every board inside it; presence = avatars of who has the board open plus an outline with the name on whatever someone is typing in or dragging (no live cursors). Built in steps: (1) combining edits, (2) sharing and syncing shared boards, (3) undo of one's own changes only, (4) presence, (5) version history of shared boards. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): edits are combined thing by thing (`src/model/merge.ts`, a three-way merge against the last version both saw) rather than with a CRDT library: no new dependency, it works on the board shape the app already has, and it is pure and unit-tested; when both change the same field the later one wins; a thing deleted on one side but edited on the other is kept (moving a block doesn't count as an edit, since blocks re-arrange themselves); two items nested inside each other lift one to the top of its list; the home board can't be shared (it holds a person's whole space). The private sync of a person's own devices keeps "most recent wins" for now.
- 2026-10-05 (editing together, step 2: sharing by link; my calls, recommended option per the owner rule): a shared board is one Firestore document `shared/{id}` (its boards as one workspace-shaped JSON, the shared board as "home"), with `members/{uid}` and each person’s list in `boards/{uid}/shared/{id}`; saving is a transaction that reads the latest, combines (`mergeBoardSets`) against the version last agreed and writes, with a save counter `rev` that the rules make go up by one; the agreed version is kept in localStorage (`note-board:share-base:{id}`) so offline changes and changes from before a reload combine correctly. The person’s own boards sync as before but without shared boards, and only once the shared ones are known (at most 5 seconds’ wait). Anyone with access sees and can copy the link (it is link sharing); turning it on again makes a new link. The owner’s delete removes it for everyone and keeps the boards inside it on the owner’s boards; removed people’s member records are deleted, but a deleted share’s member records are left (tiny, unreadable without the share; replaced 2026-10-06: they are deleted with it). The link is `?join=id.key`, taken out of the address once used; opening it signed out keeps it for after sign-in. A board someone shared can’t be renamed or deleted by the others (their Boards menu has no ×; they Leave instead), and restoring a version of a shared board is the owner’s only, as the owner chose. Locally, `npm run dev` with `?demo-user=Name` gives demo people and a pretend server (`src/sync/devServer.ts`, dev server only), used by the browser tests (`e2e/sharing.spec.ts`); `E2E_PORT` / `E2E_PREVIEW_PORT` let a second copy of the project run its browser tests at the same time. The toolbar’s dividers sit 1px from their neighbors (was 4px) in windows up to 1500px, so Share fits beside Sign out at 1280px.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request: a game-like sense of progress for the ant theme, an ant that goes higher up a hill for every checklist item Clean up sends into Completed). My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): the hill sits in the Completed card under its title, where cleaned-up items land; one step per ticked item in the card, ticked sub-items included, open sub-items not; each hill is longer than the last (10, 20, 30… steps) so it keeps feeling like a climb, and the ant waits at the flag ("Top of hill 1!") until the next item starts the next hill, appearing at its foot rather than sliding down; the count is worked out from the Completed card instead of being saved as its own number (items only leave that card by being sent back, so tidying never lowers it, and saving, sync and undo needed no change); an item sent back to its list takes its step back, since it is no longer done; the ant walks for 1–3 s with a small bob, at once with reduced motion; hidden on a collapsed card (`src/model/antHill.ts`, `AntHill.tsx`).
- 2026-10-05 (owner request: the arrow dots were too jarring and covered text): dots are 10px, a soft gray ring at 70% strength, fading in after 0.12 s (no fade with reduced motion), amber and 1.4× under the pointer. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): a dot that would lie over another card or column, or over the title strip of the column its card sits in, is left out rather than moved (the arrow picks its own sides anyway, so any remaining dot draws the same arrow); the rest of a card's own column doesn't count, so cards in a column keep their side dots; when every dot would be covered (boxed in, or zoomed far out), the least covered one stays (spec check), so an arrow can always be started; dots still show on hover, not only on selection, so arrows stay easy to find.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request: the ant hill belongs on the board itself, near the top, as a static object that can't be interacted with, not inside the Completed card). My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): "static" taken as fixed on screen at the top middle of the board, like the zoom control, so it is always in sight and doesn't move with panning or zooming (rather than pinned to one spot on the board, where it would often be out of view); it sits over the cards, under the toolbar's menus and every panel, and takes no clicks, so a card under it can still be used; it shows from the start ("Hill 1 · 0 of 10"), before there is a Completed card; each board shows its own hill (its own Completed card), and opening another board puts the ant in place without walking.
- 2026-10-05 (owner request: one agent coordinating the others, like Firstmate, which doesn't run on Windows): a `/crew` skill makes the main session the coordinator, using the desktop app's own tools instead of Firstmate. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): workers are background agents, each in its own git worktree on its own branch, finishing with `/finish-step` and a pull request into `build/v1` as any non-main session does (rule 9); at most 3 at a time on this computer; jobs are `ship` (pull request), `scout` (report only) or `session` (a desktop session the owner started, only watched); two jobs touching the same files run one after the other; the job log is `.claude/crew-log.md`, kept out of git so it never causes merge clashes and a fresh coordinator can carry on from it. Found while building: every worktree's browser tests used port 5173 and reused whatever dev server was already there, so tests could pass against another worktree's app; each linked worktree now gets its own pair of ports from its folder (`scripts/test-ports.mjs`, 5200–5499 / 4200–4499), while the main folder and CI keep 5173 / 4173, and `E2E_DEV_PORT` / `E2E_PREVIEW_PORT` override.
- 2026-10-06 (security review of sharing, job #2; my calls, recommended option per the owner rule): the review found that a share's starting board and board cards, as saved online, could make one of the person's own boards part of a share (replaced, uploaded, deleted with it). A share now holds only the boards in its own data (its starting board must be one of them, or it can't be opened), plus boards that a board card made on this device opens; a board card that came with the data (on any of its boards, or in an earlier version) never brings a board in. A board in the share's data with the id of one of the person's own boards is left out on their screen; the one exception is the owner's first opening on another device, which takes over that device's old copy of the board they shared and the boards inside it. A later version without its starting board is ignored. Also found and fixed: removing someone and changing the link are one transaction (the link changes only if it is on, read online), so the old link never lets them back in; the shares a device has are kept on it (`note-board:shares:{uid}`) so they are known as shared before the server answers (before, a slow or failed list let the person's own-board sync drop them from the device, and the share then sent their deletion to everyone); a page never sends a share without its starting board, and a starting board gone from the device comes back; the list of shares from the offline copy no longer counts as "left"; a deleted share's member records are now deleted with it (replacing the earlier "left in place" call), and a page that sees a different owner under a share's id treats it as deleted; a change made while a refused save was on its way is tried once more; own-board sync waits while a newer version waits for a drag. Left alone: a "Join this board?" prompt before joining from a link (SPEC doesn't ask for one; suggested to the owner; settled 2026-10-06: no prompt, see below); if a device can't keep its copy of a share (storage full) and still has the boards, the share can't open there for someone it was shared with. Browser tests now take their ports from `scripts/test-ports.mjs` (E2E_DEV_PORT / E2E_PREVIEW_PORT), replacing E2E_PORT.
- 2026-10-06 (owner request, after a second coordinator restarted jobs whose workers were still running): one crew coordinator at a time, named at the top of `.claude/crew-log.md`; another session becomes coordinator only when the owner says so. Before restarting a job it didn't start, a new coordinator checks the old coordinator's session, then the job's folder, and asks the owner when unsure. My call: the check lives in the `/crew` skill, not in code, since background workers can only be seen from the session that started them.
- 2026-10-06 (owner rule, narrows the 2026-10-04 one): the recommended option stays the default for questions while building, but Claude asks the owner first, with a recommendation, when unsure what the owner would want or when the choice is high-impact (could lose or expose data, hard to undo, changes saving / sync / sharing / security, needs a change only the owner can make outside the code, or changes what the owner sees in a way the spec doesn't settle). In CLAUDE.md rule 4 and the /crew skill; crew workers report such questions as BLOCKED.
- 2026-10-06 (owner request: the work board's "Needs me" should only show what truly waits on the owner): the crew log gets a "Needs you" list kept by the coordinator: `[decision]` lines for questions the owner must answer, `[do]` lines for next steps only the owner can do, removed once answered or done. The work board mod (outside the repo) shows that list plus blocked and waiting-owner crew jobs, high-risk pull requests whose tests passed, stalled pull requests (tests failed, nobody fixing), and sessions that actually asked a question; everything else stays under In progress.
- 2026-10-06 (owner request: the ant hill isn't wanted any more). Removed entirely, with no setting to bring it back; this replaces both 2026-10-05 ant hill decisions above. Nothing about it was ever saved (its count was worked out from the Completed card), so saved boards open unchanged. Clean up, the Completed card and the rest of the BusyAnts look (logo, icons, colors) stay as they were. My call: the hill's own colors (`--hill`, `--hill-edge`, `--hill-trail`, `--ant`) went with it, since nothing else used them.
- 2026-10-06 (owner decision): no "Join this board?" question. Opening a share link (and signing in, if needed) joins the shared board straight away and opens it, as built. This settles the question left open in the security review entry above.
- 2026-10-06 (owner confirmed, all three already built this way): (1) everyone a board is shared with can see and copy its link; (2) turning the link off and on again makes a new link, and old copies of it stop working; (3) deleting a shared board (only its owner can) deletes it for everyone, and the boards inside it stay with the owner.
- 2026-10-06 (main session check of pull request #15, job #2; my calls, recommended option per the owner rule): (1) a board card pasted or moved onto a shared board no longer brings in a board that was already one of the person's own: the page notes every board that a card on one of the person's own boards opens (home included; kept on the device in `note-board:own-boards:{uid}`, so a card pasted offline is still not followed after a reload), and a new board card on a shared board never takes one of those in; the card just opens the board, for that person only, and others can't open it. A sub-board made on a shared board is opened only from there, so it still joins the share, even when its card is cut and pasted onto another of the share's boards. Noted only once the shares kept on the device are open, so their own sub-boards aren't taken for private ones. (2) A shared board going from a device (removed, left, deleted, or deleted on the owner's other device, also when found out while the page opens, before version history has started: then it is saved straight to the online history) first saves every board as a version when version history is on, like every other delete; it isn't waited for (the boards are read at once), so it never holds anything up; the owner's own delete already saved one (deleteBoardSafely). (3) Own-board sync: a change made during a drag while a newer version from another device waits is now kept and uploaded when the drag ends (it is newer, as a drop already was); otherwise the waiting version is shown; "Saving…" settles either way. (4) Own-board sync started by the 5-second timer before the shares are known treats these as possibly shared, neither uploaded nor removed: a board in a share's data kept on the device (only the shares on this person's kept list, or every one kept if there is no list yet), and, after the first online version, a board in none of the person's online copies (made meanwhile). Any other board left on the device still gives way to the online copy on first load (it may be another account's), and with no online copy yet the device's boards are uploaded as before. Once the share list answers, everything is synced by the usual rule. The comparison with the last synced copy is now made in board-id order, so it no longer depends on the order boards arrive in. (Code review) a drop that changes only a shared board no longer throws away a waiting newer version of the person's own boards: only a change to their own boards counts as newer. Left alone (review): if the share list never answers while online, boards made meanwhile wait to be uploaded until it does; and on the first run of this version, another account's shares kept on the same device count as possibly shared until the list answers. (5) Leaving a shared board online is one batch (member record and list entry), so it works fully or not at all; the boards go from the device only after it worked, and the panel shows "That didn’t work. Check your connection and try again." if not. No change to `firestore.rules` was needed: a person may already delete their own member record, even of a deleted share. (6) Only a change in which boards are shared counts as a change for own-board sync, not a new list of people or link, so "Saving…" no longer flickers. (7) The Share panel button reads "Turn link on", as in SPEC; the two error notes are now in SPEC.
- 2026-10-06 (owner chose to fix two code-quality review findings on pull request #15, job #14; my calls, recommended option per the owner rule): (1) everything about "which boards might be shared while the shares aren't known yet" moved out of own-board sync into one place in the sharing code (`ownBoardsGuard` in `src/store/sharing.ts`); sync now only asks "is this board shared?" and gets told to look again once the shares are known. The guard learns which boards are in the person's online copy by watching that copy on sync's behalf, so sync no longer keeps that list. Behavior is unchanged; the five unit tests for this period kept their checks and only had their setup moved to the new boundary. (2) The Share panel now stays open until leaving a board has worked, so a failure shows its note there.
- 2026-10-06 (owner request, job #20): a keyboard shortcut deletes completed checklist items. Owner's decisions: it removes only items in Completed sections (with their sub-items), only on the open board (not other boards or sub-boards); it always asks first in the app's confirmation style ("Delete 12 completed items?" / "Delete 1 completed item?" · Keep · red Delete), and Ctrl+Z after Delete brings them all back in one step; on a shared board it is an edit like any other; no menu button or setting, only the shortcut list. My calls (recommended option, per the owner rule): the key is Ctrl+Shift+Backspace, with the Control key on a Mac too (code review found ⌘+Shift+Backspace is Chrome's and Firefox's Clear browsing data on a Mac, so ⌘ is not taken there); while it asks, other board keys wait; it does nothing while an old version is previewed; it doesn't fire while typing in a text field; the count includes sub-items, since they go too; ticked sub-items under open items and the Completed card are left alone (they aren't in a Completed section); a list left empty gets one blank item, like Clean up; the question shows centered below the toolbar (Keep has the focus, Escape keeps); with nothing completed, nothing is deleted and "No completed items on this board" shows for 2.5 seconds; it is listed in the Keyboard shortcuts panel under Checklists.

For someone a board was shared with, restoring a version or a backup file leaves every board of that share (its starting board and every board inside it) exactly as it is, and restores only their own boards; boards they shared themselves restore as before. A backup board with the same id as one of those boards is left out; a backup that would remove one keeps it. The safety version is still saved first. The rule is one pure function, `restorable` in `src/model/sharing.ts` (`boardRights` uses it), applied in the two store actions every restore goes through (`restoreVersion` and `restoreBackup`). Code review (high): a one-board backup picked while such a board is open no longer asks "Replace this board…?" (and saves a pointless version) only to change nothing: it says "Only the person who shared this board can restore it." straight away. Left alone (review): the check knows the shares only once sharing has started on the page (a restore in the second or so after opening, before that, isn't guarded; a follow-up could use the shares kept on the device); a board that was in a share but has since been deleted from it comes back from a version or backup as the person's own board, as any deleted board does (it is no longer in the share); a share whose starting board isn't on the device is named "Untitled board" in the note.

My calls: (1) plain bullet and numbered lines ("- a", "* a", "+ a", "1. a", "1) a") become unticked items when right under a heading (blank lines between allowed) or inside a list already started; anywhere else they stay part of a note, so a Milanote note with bullets in it imports as before. (2) A "---" settings block at the very top of a Markdown file (Obsidian front matter) is skipped. (3) In HTML each paragraph is its own note (a heading right above starts it); in Markdown, paragraphs next to each other still make one note, as Milanote exports need. (4) Limits: at most 300 cards and 2,000 checklist items per file; a longer file adds its first part (the list that goes over is cut) and says "That file is very long, so only its first part was added."; over 5 MB says "That file is too big to import (the limit is 5 MB)." Notes show as a browser alert, like the backup restore's. (5) A file whose content starts like HTML (<!doctype html> or <html>) is read as HTML whatever its name; a file with another name, or with binary content, is one it can't read. (6) Also skipped in HTML: noscript, template, iframe and svg. (7) Unit tests of the HTML reading run in Vitest with happy-dom (new dev-only package; nothing was set up for a DOM in tests). Board names match ignoring case and surrounding spaces, an exact id wins over a name, and an untitled board is "Untitled board". "Safe to edit" compares the saved JSON with what the app's own reader and writer make of it, ignoring key order; an old single board is "no" (open the app once to update it). The Claude Code deny rule is `Read(~/AppData/Roaming/busyants-mcp/**)` in `.claude/settings.json`; it covers Claude's file tools and the shell commands Claude Code recognizes, not other programs (the connector itself must read the file).

Item text is one line (line breaks become spaces); note text keeps its lines. Each running connector saves with its own `client` name, `mcp-` and 8 random characters, so an open app treats its saves as coming from elsewhere. An activity log that can't be written never stops a save (the reason goes to stderr); the deny rule on `%APPDATA%\busyants-mcp` covers the log too, so Claude Code can't read it (the owner opens it in Notepad). If reading back after a save fails (offline), the answer still says "Saved" and that it couldn't be checked. A change that makes no difference saves nothing and says "Nothing changed". The 50-change and 5,000-character limits are checked before anything is read, so an over-long call changes nothing.

(4) Bullets: • ● ○ ◦ ▪ ▫ ■ ‣ ⁃ ∙ · and Word's Symbol / Wingdings bullets, "-", "*", "+", "–", "—" and "1." / "1)" followed by a space; checkboxes ☐ □ "[ ]" unticked, ☑ ☒ ✓ ✔ ✅ "[x]" ticked; nesting from how far each item is indented. (6) A PDF with no text says "That PDF has no text BusyAnts can read (it may be a scan), so nothing was added."; a broken or password-locked PDF says "BusyAnts can’t read that file yet." (7) A file is a PDF by its .pdf name or its first bytes ("%PDF-"). (8) No CMap files are shipped, so text in some older East Asian PDFs may not come out (noted in the code). The shares this device kept (its share list and each share's last agreed boards) count at once. Until then a restore (a version or a backup file) does nothing at all (no safety version saved, no board opened, a backup file isn't even asked about) and says "Your shared boards are still loading, so nothing was restored. My calls: the device's kept list counts as having said (as the job asked), so a share joined on another device since is guarded only once the server's list arrives; the share list is now kept even when empty, so someone with no shares restores at once on their next visit; if the server refuses the share list, restoring isn't held back for the session (no share can arrive through it).
